## Review

**4 out of 10, Ineffective.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck is a working-meeting artifact stitched from two organizations' templates, and the audience cannot follow a single argument through it. Many pages are repeated agenda trackers (slides 15, 16, 19, 24), several are dense tables set in tiny type that cannot be read at presentation size (slides 18, 22, 23), and slides 38 and 39 show clipped or empty-looking layouts. The survey charts on slide 5 and the timeline on slide 36 are legible and carry their point, but they sit among many slides with weak hierarchy, no takeaway titles and unexplained content such as the Venn on slide 21.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the survey bar charts on slide 5 with their direct value labels and average per chart, and the color-coded workstream bars of the timeline on slide 36.

**Most useful repair:** Cut the repeated change-management tracker slides, enlarge or split the stakeholder and matrix tables on slides 17, 18, 22 and 23, and fix the overlapping footers and the clipped table on slide 38.

**What would change the score:** I would rebuild the deck as one template with action titles that state each page's conclusion, so a reader gets a clear status, decisions needed and next steps without a presenter.

_Confidence high. 39 of 39 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-09. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

This confidential internal partnership meeting deck, dated 6 August 2014, was prepared by Theranos for its retail pharmacy partner Walgreens. It opens with a title slide and a numbered agenda covering kick-off, current status, anchored values, success measures, the FY15 plan, California plans and standard testing. The status section presents store counts, launch waves and visit-time medians as plain bullets. A survey summary follows, with a headline stat slide, star-rating bar charts and pie charts for prescription sources and likelihood to return. Next comes a quote slide of patient comments, then tables of shortest and longest visit times by store. The opportunities section lists operational priorities, and a technician certification slide sets removal and reinstatement criteria. The Walgreens-branded middle section includes a business and change considerations matrix, a stakeholder model and a SMART goals table with owners and targets. The closing sections cover the FY15 market plan, a California T-minus schedule with a timeline graphic, and an IT phase plan with its scope. The deck ends with its phase 3 priorities.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides does the Theranos partnership meeting deck have?**

The Theranos partnership meeting deck, dated 6 August 2014, is listed as having 39 pages. Slide 1 is the title slide, which opens the deck.

**What does the Theranos deck say about current store status and median check-in time?**

Slide 3 states that 30 stores are currently live (AZ 29; CA 1) and that the median check-in time is 5 min, a decrease of 1 min. The same slide reports total visits in August of 2637.

**What does the Theranos survey summary report for overall experience?**

Slide 4 reports 650 unique respondents for the period July 2 to August 4, 2014, with an average overall experience score of 4.85 stars. Slide 5 shows the bar chart for this overall experience rating.

**What success criteria does the Theranos SMART goals slide list for patient experience?**

Slide 27 lists a likelihood to come back of 90% or more by 8/31/15 and a referral by a friend or family member of 20% or more by 8/31/15. The same slide sets a training pass rate of 90% or more by 8/31/15.

**Layout**

A single centred column of text on a white slide. The title sits at the top centre with an italic date line beneath it, followed by a centred 'Unique Respondents' block and a centred 'Average Quality/Experience Scores' heading. Below that, the six labelled scores form two left-aligned columns: labels starting at about x 31.5% and values starting at about x 61.5%. The footer has 'Theranos Confidential' at bottom left, the 'Trial Exh. 1884 Page 0020' stamp at bottom centre, and the theranos logo at bottom right. No slide number is shown.

**Visuals**

None apart from the theranos wordmark logo at bottom right: dark grey lowercase letters 'theranos' with the 'o' replaced by a teal-green dot. It is a small raster image (104 by 19 pixels), not cropped, not boxed, with no overlay.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background. Titles and accent headings are in Arial Bold, body text in Arial regular, black. Accent headings are teal-green #00B28C. Title is black Arial Bold at about 7.8% of slide height, with the date line in Arial Italic at about 3.3%. Body and score text is Arial regular at about 3.3%. Footer text is Arial at about 2.2%, and the Trial Exh. stamp is at about 1.5%. No line, shape, corner radius, shadow or gradient is present.

### Slide 5

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Small multiple bar charts with consistent axes and a single highlighted average give the survey results a clear, scannable form.
- Review: The survey charts on slide 5 and the timeline on slide 36 are legible and carry their point, but they sit among many slides with weak hierarchy, no takeaway titles and unexplained content such as the Venn on slide 21.
- Worth keeping: Keep the survey bar charts on slide 5 with their direct value labels and average per chart, and the color-coded workstream bars of the timeline on slide 36.

**Layout**

Four equal chart panels in a 2 by 2 grid on a white field: Overall Experience top left, Check In Process top right, Locating Theranos bottom left, Facilities bottom right. Each panel has a centred bold green title above a vertical bar chart, with a centred bold 'Average' line beneath it. Footer: 'Theranos Confidential' bottom left, centred small 'Trial Exh. 1884 Page 0021' near bottom centre, and the theranos logo bottom right. No slide number is shown.

**Visuals**

Four vertical bar charts, each with 6 bars for star ratings 0 to 5 (x-axis label values 0,1,2,3,4,5; no axis titles, no units shown beyond stars in the averages). Y-axis gridlines light grey. Bars solid cyan blue #00A8E1 with no outlines; each bar carries a bold black value label above it. Chart 1 Overall Experience: y-axis 0 to 600 in steps of 100; values 0:1, 1:4, 2:4, 3:6, 4:47, 5:556; average 4.85 stars. Chart 2 Check In Process: y-axis 0 to 600 in steps of 100; values 0:1, 1:11, 2:13, 3:41, 4:81, 5:497; average 4.61 stars. Chart 3 Locating Theranos: y-axis 0 to 600 in steps of 100; values 0:0, 1:5, 2:6, 3:20, 4:51, 5:534; average 4.79 stars. Chart 4 Facilities: y-axis 0 to 200 in steps of 20; values 0:0, 1:2, 2:2, 3:5, 4:25, 5:194; average 4.79 stars. No legend. No highlighted points beyond the 5-star bar, which is tallest in each chart. The theranos logo is a wordmark in dark grey with a green dot replacing the o, boxed by nothing.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background #FFFFFF with no decoration. Titles in bold sans-serif (Arial Bold) in green #00B28C, about 3.3 percent of slide height. Average lines in bold Arial black #000000, about 2.6 percent of slide height. Axis and value labels in Arial regular or bold black #000000, about 1.85 percent of slide height. Bars #00A8E1 flat, no shadows, no gradients. Gridlines thin light grey. Footer text in Arial regular black #000000 about 2.2 percent (Theranos Confidential) and 1.5 percent (Trial Exh line).

**Layout**

Two equal-width bar charts sit side by side in the upper half of a white slide, each centred under its own title and above its own average line; the left chart spans roughly x 15 to 44 percent and the right roughly x 56 to 85 percent. The lower half of the slide is empty. A footer row holds 'Theranos Confidential' at bottom left, a small centred document label, and the theranos logo at bottom right, with margins of about 2 percent.

**Visuals**

Two vertical bar charts. Left chart titled 'Sample Collection Process': y-axis 0 to 600 in steps of 100 with gridlines; x-axis categories 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5 (star ratings). Values per bar: 0 = 1, 1 = 5, 2 = 2, 3 = 10, 4 = 36, 5 = 558 (value labels printed above each bar, bold). Right chart titled 'Skill of Technician': y-axis 0 to 700 in steps of 100 with gridlines; x-axis categories 0 to 5. Values: 0 = 1, 1 = 5, 2 = 1, 3 = 7, 4 = 22, 5 = 577 (value labels printed above each bar, bold). Bars are solid #00A8E1 with no outline; no legend; no highlighted points. Below each chart is a bold average line: 'Average: 4.86 stars' (left) and 'Average: 4.90 stars' (right). Small theranos logo at bottom right, a wordmark in dark grey with a teal-green dot in place of the o.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white slide with no decoration. Chart titles are bold sans-serif (Arial Bold) in green #00B28C at about 3.3 percent of slide height, centred. Bars and accents are cyan-blue #00A8E1. Body and axis labels are black #000000 in Arial regular at about 1.85 percent of slide height; value labels and average lines are Arial Bold, the average lines at about 2.6 percent. Axis labels are regular weight; gridlines are light grey hairlines. No shapes with rounded corners, no shadows, no gradients.

Trial Exh. 1884 Page 0023

**Layout**

Single-slide content layout with the title flush left near the top, a pie chart in the left-centre of the body, and a vertical legend in a column to the right of the pie. The footer row sits at the bottom: 'Theranos Confidential' at bottom left, the trial exhibit line centred near the bottom, and the theranos logo at bottom right. No slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

One pie chart with no title of its own, no axes and no legend box beyond the vertical list at right. Eight slices, clockwise from 12 o'clock: Walgreens 53% (#00B28C, green, largest slice, spanning from 12 o'clock around the right and bottom to about 8 o'clock); CVS 15% (#00A8E1, blue); Walmart 12% (#FFDD00, yellow); Costco 7% (#FF671B, orange); Target 3% (#E6E7E8, light grey); Safeway 3% (#A7A9AC, mid grey); Rite-Aid 1% (#C00000, dark red, thin sliver); Other 7% (#8CE1FF, light sky blue, ending at 12 o'clock). Percentage labels are printed in bold black Arial on each slice: 53%, 15%, 12%, 7% (Costco), 3% (Target), 3% (Safeway), 1% (Rite-Aid), 7% (Other); the 1%, 3% and 3% labels sit close together near the top-left edge of the small slices. Legend at right lists in order Walgreens, CVS, Walmart, Costco, Target, Safeway, Rite-Aid, Other, each with a small square swatch in the slice colour. Slices have thin white or hairline separators. The theranos logo is a raster image (104 by 19 pixels): dark grey wordmark 'theranos' with a teal-green dot replacing the 'o'.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background. Title in bold black Arial, roughly 4.4 percent of slide height for capitals-height line size as measured, left aligned. Body and legend text in regular black Arial at about 2.6 percent of slide height. Footer 'Theranos Confidential' in regular black Arial about 2.2 percent; trial exhibit line in Arial about 1.5 percent. Pie slices use flat fills with no shadows or gradients and hairline separators. Accent colours are the slice colours: green #00B28C, blue #00A8E1, yellow #FFDD00, orange #FF671B, light grey #E6E7E8, mid grey #A7A9AC, red #C00000, light blue #8CE1FF. No boxes or borders around any element.

**Layout**

Single-slide chart layout on a white field: a bold two-line title is left-aligned at the top-left margin (about 2.1% from left, 8.4% from top). A pie chart sits in the centre-left of the slide with a vertical legend to its right, aligned to the right half. A footer row runs along the bottom: 'Theranos Confidential' at bottom-left, a centred small Bates-style line 'Trial Exh. 1884 Page 0024' near the bottom centre, and the theranos logo at bottom-right. No slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Pie chart, no printed chart title beyond the slide headline. Three slices, clockwise from 12 o'clock: 'Definitely coming back' 82% (green slice, the largest, covering most of the right and bottom-left of the circle), 'Likely to return' 17% (blue slice, upper-left quadrant between about 10 and 12 o'clock), 'Not sure if I will return' 1% (thin yellow slice just left of 12 o'clock). Percent labels are printed in black bold sans-serif inside or near each slice: 1% near the yellow slice, 17% inside the blue slice, 82% inside the green slice near the lower-right edge. No axes. Legend on the right with square colour swatches: green swatch beside 'Definitely coming back', blue swatch beside 'Likely to return', yellow swatch beside 'Not sure if I will return', listed top to bottom with even spacing. No highlighted points. Slice boundaries are thin, same-hue or hairline edges. Logo: the theranos wordmark in dark grey lowercase sans-serif with a teal-green gradient dot replacing the 'o', at bottom-right, about 8.1% wide.

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF.

### Slide 9

**Text**

Here’s what people are saying…
“Absolutely love the new technology Theranos has for blood tests. I will definitely let all my family and friends know about
your company.”
“Another fabulous experience! I didn't feel any pain, the procedure was so quick, and Tiffany was a delight! Thank you for
making the process of giving blood so easy and pleasant.”
“Best visit I have ever had. Amazing people they take really amazing care of people.”
“For a person who has severe phobia of needles and blood this was a pleasant experience all around.”
“This was the easiest lab test blood draw I’ve done in my life!”
“Fantastic experience! I couldn't even feel my skin being pierced; it was so easy. Thank you for inventing this to help people!”
“Easy and convenient! Can't beat the price.”
“Great customer service was provided at the Walgreens location lab today!”
Theranos Confidential
Trial Exh. 1884 Page 0025
theranos

### Slide 11

**Text**

longest visit times / Name / Median Checkin Time (mins) / WAG2851 / 7 / WAG3008 / 7 / WAG4793 / 7 / WAG6060 / 7 / WAG6128 / 7 / WAG6697 / 7 / WAG1076 / 8 / WAG4188 / 8 / WAG4508 / 9 / WAG6582 / 9 / Name / Median Wait Time (mins) / WAG3048 / 8 / WAG3049 / 8 / WAG3177 / 8 / WAG3464 / 8 / WAG3912 / 8 / WAG6128 / 8 / WAG4793 / 9 / WAG3008 / 10 / WAG1076 / 12 / WAG6697 / 20 / Name / Median Perform Time (mins) / WAG6128 / 8 / WAG3912 / 9 / WAG5222 / 9 / WAG5504 / 9.5 / WAG4508 / 10 / WAG4793 / 10 / WAG6697 / 12 / Trial Exh. 1884 Page 0027 / theranos confidential / 11

**Layout**

Three-column table layout with the title flush left at the top, about 4% from the left edge, and three tables side by side in the middle band, left table starting about 8% from the left edge, middle at about 39%, right at about 70%. The tables are top-aligned at unequal heights because each column header wraps to a different number of lines. A centred small 'Trial Exh. 1884 Page 0027' line sits near the bottom centre, a grey 'theranos confidential' footer sits bottom left, a centred page number '11' sits bottom centre, and the theranos logo sits bottom right.

**Visuals**

Three plain tables with no fills, no cell borders except a thin grey rule under each header row. Table 1 has columns Name and Median Checkin Time (mins) with rows WAG2851 7, WAG3008 7, WAG4793 7, WAG6060 7, WAG6128 7, WAG6697 7, WAG1076 8, WAG4188 8, WAG4508 9, WAG6582 9. Table 2 has columns Name and Median Wait Time (mins) with rows WAG3048 8, WAG3049 8, WAG3177 8, WAG3464 8, WAG3912 8, WAG6128 8, WAG4793 9, WAG3008 10, WAG1076 12, WAG6697 20. Table 3 has columns Name and Median Perform Time (mins) with rows WAG6128 8, WAG3912 9, WAG5222 9, WAG5504 9.5, WAG4508 10, WAG4793 10, WAG6697 12. Numeric values are right-aligned within their column. Row spacing is roughly 4.5% of slide height in the first table and slightly irregular in the others. One theranos wordmark logo at bottom right, dark grey lettering with a teal-green dot replacing the letter o, 138 by 26 px.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background. Title in regular Arial/Helvetica-style grotesque sans-serif, grey #646569, size about 6.9% of slide height, not bold. Table text in Arial regular, dark grey #333132, about 2.8% of slide height; column headers in Arial Bold #333132 at the same size, set on multiple lines. Header rules are thin light grey lines about #9E9FA0. Footer text in Arial regular about 1.5% of slide height, grey #646569; page number in lighter grey #9E9FA0; Trial Exh. line in black Arial about 1.5%. No shapes, shadows, gradients or corner radii.

### Slide 12

**Text**

opportunities / Sunny / ● scalable strategy for check in / ● store by store – start with stores with 10+ patients per day / ● must start in August - will be tested during flu season / ● tight integration between HCC and Theranos check-in / ● call center knowledge of Theranos and communication to patients / ● assurance that 40% of stores will be dedicated space / ● improved staffing in all stores – surprises with Theranos tech shortages / ● need improvement in attentiveness of some stores / ● cleaning / ● attention to process – including opening and closing procedures / ● managing flow of patients / Trial Exh. 1884 Page 0028 / theranos confidential / 12 / theranos

**Layout**

Single left-aligned text column on a white field with a flat, unboxed layout. The title 'opportunities' sits top-left at about 4% left margin, with the italic subtitle 'Sunny' directly beneath it. The bullet list starts about 10% from the left, with its bullet markers at 10.4% and text at 14.2%, and runs down to about 90% of the height. A centred Bates-style line sits near the bottom at about 45% from the left, the 'theranos confidential' footer sits bottom-left, the page number '12' sits bottom-centre, and the Theranos wordmark sits bottom-right.

**Visuals**

None apart from the corner logo: a Theranos wordmark at bottom right (x 86.7%, y 93.9%, 10.8% by 3.6% of the slide, 138 by 26 px). The word 'theranos' is set in a bold grey sans-serif, approximately #646569, with the letter 'o' replaced by a solid teal-green sphere, approximately #3BB39A, that sits on the baseline. The logo is unboxed and not cropped.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient, texture or shadows. Title 'opportunities' is in a regular-weight Arial-style grotesque sans-serif, colour #646569, at about 6.9% of slide height. The subtitle 'Sunny' is Arial Italic at about 4.9%, colour #646569. Body bullets are Arial regular, colour #646569, at about 4.9% of slide height. Sub-bullets are Arial regular at about 3.0%, colour #646569. Bullet markers are solid round dots in the same grey. Footer text is Arial at about 1.5%. The 'Trial Exh.' line is black #000000 and the 'theranos confidential' line is #646569. The page number is #9E9FA0. Accent colour is the teal #3BB39A used in the logo. There are no rules, shape fills, corner radii or shadows.

None apart from the theranos wordmark logo at bottom right: dark grey lettering reading 'theranos' in a bold sans-serif, with the letter o replaced by a glossy teal-green sphere (about #2BB59A) and a dark grey #5A5C5F wordmark; the logo is a raster image 138 by 26 pixels, unboxed and not cropped.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient, shadows or decoration. Title, headings and bullet text are grey #646569 in regular-weight Arial (ArialMT); the title is the largest text at about 6.9 percent of slide height, numbered headings about 4.9 percent and bullets about 3.5 percent. Bullets are filled round dots (SymbolMT) and the fourth heading uses a Wingdings diamond glyph; there are no lines, boxes or corner radii.

### Slide 14

**Text**

Diagnostic Testing – Theranos Partnership Change
Management Updates and Key Anchors Readout
• August 6, 2014
©2014 Walgreen Co. All rights reserved. Confidential and proprietary information. For internal use only.
Trial Exh. 1884 Page 0030

**Layout**

Single-column title slide on a white rounded panel inset within a blue border; the title block sits left-aligned at about 14.8% from the left, in the upper-middle of the panel, with a bullet date line below it. The Change Management logo sits top-left and the Walgreens tagline logo sits bottom-right, with a copyright footer line bottom-left and a Bates-style exhibit label beneath it, overlapping slightly at the right end of the copyright line.

**Visuals**

Logos only: (1) Walgreens red speech-bubble 'W' mark with a small trademark symbol, followed by the orange-and-grey 'CHANGE MANAGEMENT' wordmark with a stacked orange triangular stripe icon; (2) Walgreens red script wordmark over the tagline 'AT THE CORNER OF HAPPY & HEALTHY' in grey with green 'HAPPY' and blue 'HEALTHY'. The slide has no charts, photographs or diagrams; the rounded white panel and blue frame are background treatment.

**Design**

Background is a sky-blue frame (#56A0D3) surrounding a white (#FFFFFF) rounded-corner panel with corner radius about 2% of slide width and no visible shadow. Title and bullet text are dark grey (#4D4D4D) in Arial/Helvetica regular weight; title text is about 5.6% of slide height per line (cap height roughly 3.9%), body bullet about 4.1% of slide height. **Layout**

A full-width blue header band across the top 16.7% of the slide holds the white title, left-aligned at about 6% from the left edge. Below it, a single left-aligned bullet column starts at about 8.3% and uses two indent levels, with level-one items in dark grey and level-two items in lighter grey. The lower-left holds the logo, a centred 'Trial Exh.' stamp sits above a copyright line that is left-aligned at about 24.3%, and the page number is right-aligned at about 93.3% on the footer baseline.

**Visuals**

The only non-text visuals are the header band and the lower-left logo. The header is a flat, solid blue rectangle (image 1280 by 120 pixels) running full-bleed across the top 16.7% of the slide with no gradient, photograph or pattern. The logo is a red rounded square containing a white stylised 'w' mark with a small red tail at its lower-left, followed by a small orange chevron above the words 'CHANGE' and 'MANAGEMENT' in bold dark grey capitals stacked on two lines. No photographs, charts, tables or diagrams are present.

**Design**

Background is plain white #FFFFFF. The header band is a flat medium blue, approximately #58A0D2. The title is white #FFFFFF in a regular-weight sans-serif (Arial/Helvetica), about 5.2% of slide height, with no capitals or letter-spacing changes. Level-one bullets and text are dark grey #58595B in regular-weight Arial at about 4.4% of slide height. Level-two bullets and text are mid grey #6A737B in regular-weight Arial at about 3.7% of slide height. Footer text is Arial at about 1.5% of slide height: the stamp is black #000000 and the copyright and page number are grey #888888. Bullets are solid round dots. There are no visible shapes, borders, corner radii, shadows or gradients.

### Slide 17

**Review notes**

- Suggested repair: Cut the repeated change-management tracker slides, enlarge or split the stakeholder and matrix tables on slides 17, 18, 22 and 23, and fix the overlapping footers and the clipped table on slide 38.

**Text**

We Started Here: Business & Change Considerations Matrix Based on / Stakeholder Feedback
Recommended Focus Areas
Red = Short Term Recommended Actions
Purple = Both ST and LT Recommended Actions
Black = Long Term Recommended Actions
Definition of Success
Culture
Role Clarity
Business Readiness
Business Levers
Business Operations & Governance
• Clearly document decision rights & governance approach for partnership
• Further define short and long term success criteria
• Align business metrics to key behaviors
• Identify potential incentive alignment opportunities
• Reset “Pilot” vs. ““Initial Launch Market” expectations
• Identify common values shared by Walgreens and Theranos that can be leveraged to define success criteria
• Develop day to day interaction model that better pairs individuals across the partnership
• Define/align on Field, Corporate, and Theranos roles and responsibilities in the partnership
• Redefine/clarify roles for Kickoff Market vs. scale
• Further align governing bodies to decision making rights and escalation process
• Develop holistic Store Readiness checklist
• Define Pharmacy Workflow to incorporate scripts, WE, Theranos, and immunization services
• Develop pre-defined non-emergency SOP and app update calendar
• Define leading indicators to predict Program success
• Define scalable business model
Change Levers
Stakeholder Engagement, Communications, Organizational Alignment, and Training
• Align on communications strategy, plan, and execution with Theranos and field leadership
• Develop and measure additional training success criteria beyond pass rate
• Reframe “Pilot” vs. ““Initial Launch Market” language
• Develop and rally group behind both strategic and operational-level shared vision for the partnership
• Continue to incorporate Walgreens training principles, feedback and talent pool into training
• Leverage communications strategy recommendations (TBD)/agree on senders by message type
• Identify and develop district and store champions in existing and target markets
• Better align market/district leadership roles to training goals and focused value-add training activities
• Further enable TIFT team to realize team goals
• Facilitate SOP release approach alignment
• Identify best training practices and lessons learned from Kickoff Market to apply to scale
• Communicate Pharmacy Workflow to incorporate scripts, WE, Theranos, and immunization services
• Refine “T-minus” comms, Field Leadership Toolkit, and scenario planning exercises to better prepare field
• Measure business readiness based on lagging and leading indicators; recommend corrective actions

17

**Layout**

A full-width blue title band runs across the top with the two-line title left-aligned at about 6% from the left edge. Below it a matrix runs in columns: a narrow left label column and four equal focus-area columns (Definition of Success, Culture, Role Clarity, Business Readiness), with two stacked rows (Business Operations & Governance; Stakeholder Engagement, Communications, Organizational Alignment, and Training) and rotated row labels in the far-left margin. The legend sits top-left above the row labels, the 'Recommended Focus Areas' heading is centred over the four columns, and a footer row holds the logo at bottom-left, a centred 'Trial Exh.' line, the copyright line and the slide number at bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Table/matrix with 4 focus-area columns and 2 rows. Column headers (blue #56A0D3 cells): Definition of Success; Culture; Role Clarity; Business Readiness. Row 1 (Business Levers, light blue #D0DFEE): Definition of Success: Clearly document decision rights & governance approach for partnership (purple); Further define short and long term success criteria (black); Align business metrics to key behaviors (black); Identify potential incentive alignment opportunities (black). Culture: Reset “Pilot” vs. ““Initial Launch Market” expectations (red); Identify common values shared by Walgreens and Theranos that can be leveraged to define success criteria (purple); Develop day to day interaction model that better pairs individuals across the partnership (black). Role Clarity: Define/align on Field, Corporate, and Theranos roles and responsibilities in the partnership (red); Redefine/clarify roles for Kickoff Market vs. scale (black); Further align governing bodies to decision making rights and escalation process (black). Business Readiness: Develop holistic Store Readiness checklist (purple); Define Pharmacy Workflow to incorporate scripts, WE, Theranos, and immunization services (purple); Develop pre-defined non-emergency SOP and app update calendar (purple); Define leading indicators to predict Program success (black); Define scalable business model (black). Row 2 (Change Levers, pale #E9EFF7): Definition of Success: Align on communications strategy, plan, and execution with Theranos and field leadership (purple); Develop and measure additional training success criteria beyond pass rate (black). Culture: Reframe “Pilot” vs. ““Initial Launch Market” language (red); Develop and rally group behind both strategic and operational-level shared vision for the partnership (purple); Continue to incorporate Walgreens training principles, feedback and talent pool into training (purple). Role Clarity: Leverage communications strategy recommendations (TBD)/agree on senders by message type (red); Identify and develop district and store champions in existing and target markets (purple); Better align market/district leadership roles to training goals and focused value-add training activities (purple). Business Readiness: Further enable TIFT team to realize team goals (red); Facilitate SOP release approach alignment (purple); Identify best training practices and lessons learned from Kickoff Market to apply to scale (purple); Communicate Pharmacy Workflow to incorporate scripts, WE, Theranos, and immunization services (purple); Refine “T-minus” comms, Field Leadership Toolkit, and scenario planning exercises to better prepare field (purple); Measure business readiness based on lagging and leading indicators; recommend corrective actions (black). Colour key (legend, not a graphic): Red = Short Term Recommended Actions; Purple = Both ST and LT Recommended Actions; Black = Long Term Recommended Actions. Logo at bottom-left: the Walgreens red speech-bubble icon with a white stylised 'w' script, next to an orange chevron mark and the grey wordmark 'CHANGE MANAGEMENT' in two lines (CHANGE above MANAGEMENT). No photographs or illustrations.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Blue title band (#56A0D3) across the top about 16.7% of slide height with white Arial-style sans-serif regular title, about 4.4% of slide height per line (cap height about 3.1%). Recommended Focus Areas heading is bold italic Calibri, about 3.7% of slide height, black. Column header labels are bold Calibri in white, about 3.0% of slide height, on #56A0D3 blocks with thin white gutters between cells. ### Slide 18

**Review notes**

- Review: Many pages are repeated agenda trackers (slides 15, 16, 19, 24), several are dense tables set in tiny type that cannot be read at presentation size (slides 18, 22, 23), and slides 38 and 39 show clipped or empty-looking layouts.
- Suggested repair: Cut the repeated change-management tracker slides, enlarge or split the stakeholder and matrix tables on slides 17, 18, 22 and 23, and fix the overlapping footers and the clipped table on slide 38.

### Slide 21

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Three anchor words sit in a simple triangular arrangement with a short framing question, a restrained way to present a core message.
- Review: The survey charts on slide 5 and the timeline on slide 36 are legible and carry their point, but they sit among many slides with weak hierarchy, no takeaway titles and unexplained content such as the Venn on slide 21.

**Text**

Key Anchors Discussion / What could we ask a Pharmacy Technician to rally around, that is also meaningful to senior / leaders and others in our diverse stakeholder audience? / The common themes we agreed upon: / Pharmacy / Transformation / Patient Experience / [Scalable & Standardized] / Partnership / Relationship / Trial Exh. 1884 Page 0037 / ©2014 Walgreen Co. All rights reserved. Confidential and proprietary information. For internal use only. / 21

A full-width blue header band across the top, with the slide title left-aligned at about 6% from the left edge. The footer carries the Walgreens and Change Management logo at lower left, a centered exhibit line above a copyright line, and the slide number at lower right.

**Visuals**

Three overlapping ellipses forming a diagram. Left: a solid dark navy ellipse (#1E4164) at about x 11.1%, y 60.6%, w 24%, h 32%, containing the text 'Pharmacy / Transformation' in light blue. Centre: a larger ellipse at about x 31.7%, y 44.3%, w 33%, h 44%, filled navy #1E4164 at 60% opacity over a photographic network-of-nodes texture (thin connected lines and dot nodes in lighter blue-grey on blue facets), overlapping the left and right ellipses; it contains bold white 'Patient Experience' and white bold bracketed '[Scalable & Standardized]'. Right: an ellipse at about x 70%, y 43%, w 25%, h 34%, filled with a fine grey dot/hatch pattern (light grey on white), containing 'Partnership / Relationship' in navy text, which overlaps the centre ellipse's right edge. The logo is a red Walgreens 'W' mark in a speech-bubble shape with the grey 'CHANGE MANAGEMENT' wordmark featuring an orange triangle. No photographs apart from the network texture.

**Layout**

A full-width solid blue header band across the top 16.7% of the slide holds a two-line, left-aligned white title starting at x 6%, with the page content below it on a white field. A single full-width table runs from x 2.2% to 98.2% and y 18.5% to 96.1%, with five columns (stakeholders, role, vision level, do/say/own) and a header row; a red logo sits bottom-left at the margin, a centred-left 'Trial Exh.' stamp overlaps the table bottom, a grey copyright line sits centred-left in the footer and the page number is right-aligned at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

Table with five header cells and seven body rows. Header row (blue #56A0D3, white Calibri Bold): 'Key Stakeholders like…' spanning the first two columns, 'whose role is to…', '…the Vision at the…', 'Should do/say/own'. Row 1: stakeholders Brad Wasson, Rick Gates, Jeff Koziel, Nimesh Jhaveri | Elizabeth Holmes, Sunny Balwani | Define | Company level | Do:/Say:/Own:. Row 2: Nimesh Jhaveri, Casey Kozlowski, Mahesh Raju | Sunny Balwani, Tracy Masson, Joe Ahdoot, Christian Holmes | Define | Program/ Partnership level | Do:/Say:/Own:. Row 3: Matt Sesto, Brian Sizemore, Casey Kozlowski, Mahesh Raju | Tracy Masson, Kim Alfonso, Joe Ahdoot | Translate and reinforce | Kickoff Market level | Do:/Say:/Own:. Row 4: DMs, RXSs | Sonia Cendejas, Operations Mgrs, Theranos Sales | Translate and reinforce | District level | Do:/Say:/Own:. Row 5: CLs, TIFT Team | Sonia Cendejas, Operations Mgrs | Execute | Community level | Do:/Say:/Own:. Row 6: MGRs, RXMs, Pharmacy Team | Theranos Phlebotomists, Operations Mgrs | Execute | Store level | Do:/Say:/Own:. Row 7: IT, Finance, Call Center, Marketing | Theranos IT, Theranos Call Center | Support | Company, Program, and Field levels | Do:/Say:/Own:. Body rows alternate between #D0DFEE and #E9EFF7 fills with thin white gridlines. No photographs, charts or icons; the red logo mark at bottom left is the only graphic besides the blue band.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with a solid blue header band (#56A0D3) across the top. Title is white (#FFFFFF) Arial regular, left aligned, about 4.07% of slide height per line, two lines. Table header text is white (#FFFFFF) Calibri Bold, about 2.96% of slide height. Table body text is black (#000000) Calibri regular, about 1.85% of slide height, with 'Do:', 'Say:', 'Own:' in Calibri Bold. Body row fills alternate light blue #D0DFEE and pale blue #E9EFF7 with white (#FFFFFF) separators; header cell fills are #56A0D3. Footer copyright is Arial grey #888888 at about 1.48% height; 'Trial Exh.' stamp is Arial black 1.48%. Square corners, no shadows, no gradients.

### Slide 23

**Review notes**

- Review: Many pages are repeated agenda trackers (slides 15, 16, 19, 24), several are dense tables set in tiny type that cannot be read at presentation size (slides 18, 22, 23), and slides 38 and 39 show clipped or empty-looking layouts.
- Suggested repair: Cut the repeated change-management tracker slides, enlarge or split the stakeholder and matrix tables on slides 17, 18, 22 and 23, and fix the overlapping footers and the clipped table on slide 38.

**Text**

An Example: CLs, whose role is to execute the vision of a Superior Patient Experience; support the / Partnership Collaboration and drive Pharmacy Transformation at the Community and Store Level, / should… / Key Stakeholders like… / whose role is to… / …the Vision at the… / Should do/say/own / CLs / MGRs / RXMs / Pharmacy Team / Theranos Phlebotomists / Execute / Store level / Do: / Say: / Own: / CLs, whose role in Theranos is to execute the vision of a Superior Patient Experience and support the Partnership Collaboration at the Community and Store Level, should… / DO: / • Communicate about Theranos services and their benefits in the stores where it is offered; ask team members what they think about the service [Patient Experience, Partnership Collaboration] / • Ensure all the stores in your community are aware of Theranos services and where they are offered [Patient Experience, Partnership Collaboration] / • Regularly update and energize team members - celebrate positive patient experience results in your community [Patient Experience] / • Hold Store Managers in your community accountable for their store-level responsibilities; understand those responsibilities and ask about them on your Store Walks [Partnership Collaboration] / SAY: / • Have you gotten your lipid profile through Theranos yet? ### Slide 26

**Text**

SMART Program Measures for Success
SMART goals are defined as:
Specific: target a specific area for improvement
Measurable: quantify or at least suggest an indicator of progress
Achievable: realistic and attainable
Realistic: state what results can realistically be achieved, given available resources
Time-related: specify when the result(s) can be achieved
©2014 Walgreen Co. All rights reserved. Confidential and proprietary information. For internal use only.
Trial Exh. 1884 Page 0042
26

**Layout**

Single-column text slide with a full-width blue title band across the top (about the top 16.7% of the slide). The title sits left-aligned inside the band at about 6% from the left edge. Body text is left-aligned in one column starting at about 8.2% from the left, with five stacked lines of bullet-style definitions below the heading. The footer runs along the bottom with the Walgreens logo at bottom left, the copyright line and a Bates stamp beside it, and the slide number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Logo: the red Walgreens 'W' logo (script 'w' in white inside a red rounded speech-bubble shape with a pointed lower-left tail) at bottom left, about 3.6% wide by 5.0% high, placed at x 6.1%, y 93.3%. No photographs, charts, or diagrams.

**Design**

Background white #FFFFFF. Title band is a solid flat blue #58A0D3 across the full width, height about 16.7% of slide height. Title text white #FFFFFF in regular-weight Arial (sans-serif), size about 4.4% of slide height, sentence-case. Body text dark grey #58595B in Arial, with 'SMART goals are defined as:' in bold at about 3.7% of slide height; the five definition lines use bold italic for the key term (Specific, Measurable, Achievable, Realistic, Time-related) followed by a bold colon and regular-weight text. Line spacing is generous, about 6.7% of slide height between baselines. Footer copyright text in black #000000 Arial about 1.5% of slide height; page number in grey #898989 Arial about 1.5%. No shapes, shadows, gradients, or corner radii apart from the logo's rounded speech-bubble form.

Row 3: Category 'Patient Experience'; Goal 'Likelihood to come back by 8/31/15'; Success Criteria '=>90%'; Owner blank. Row 6: Category blank; Goal 'Conversion of non-Walgreens patients to become Walgreens patients'; Success Criteria '10% of the 47% benchmark'; Owner blank. Header cells are filled #56A0D3 with white bold Calibri; body rows alternate #D0DFEE and #E9EFF7 with white cell gutters between cells. Also a small Walgreens logo at bottom-left: a red speech-bubble shape with a white stylised 'w' mark, about 6% wide.

**Design**

**Layout**

Full-width blue title banner across the top, with the title left-aligned at about 6% from the left edge and vertically centred in the band. A four-column table starts at about 17.8% height with a header row and four body rows, spanning from about 1.1% to 98.6% width. The footer has the Walgreens logo at bottom left, the copyright line starting at about 14.5% width, a 'Trial Exh.' line centred near 45% to 55% width, and the page number at bottom right. Column widths are about 15.5%, 33.2%, 24.4% and 24.4% of the slide width.

**Visuals**

A table with four columns (Category, Goal, Success Criteria, Owner(s)) and a header row plus four body rows. Header row, bold white Calibri on #56A0D3: Category | Goal | Success Criteria | Owner(s). Row 1: IT | Theranos PSC application uptime =>99% / Note: applicable to DSL connected stores only | (blank) | (blank). Row 2: Payor and Providers | Payor coverage of at least 66% | Payor coverage of at least 66% | (blank). Row 3: Financial | Achieve 15* patients/store/day for a store that has been open at least 3 months by 8/31/15 | Achieve 15 patients/store/day | (blank). Row 4: HCC | % of lab orders driven from HCC | This is not being driven due to steerage | Sunny Balwani/ Pat Carroll. Body rows alternate #D0DFEE and #E9EFF7 with white gutters between cells. The only other graphic is the small red Walgreens speech-bubble logo at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Blue banner #56A0D3 with white sans-serif title (Arial regular, about 4.4% of slide height). Table header text is bold white Calibri at about 3.7% of slide height, with body text black Calibri at about 3.0% of slide height. Body text is black #000000. Cell fills are flat with no borders, only thin white gutters. Footer text is Arial at about 1.5% of slide height; the page number is grey #898989. No shadows, gradients or rounded corners.

**Layout**

A full-width blue header band runs across the top with the title left-aligned inside it, vertically centred in the band with a left margin of about 6%. Below it a four-column table fills the slide from about 1% to 99% of width, starting at 17.5% of height, with a header row and two body rows; the footer sits at the bottom left with the logo at about 6% from the left edge and the copyright line beside it, with no slide number visible.

**Visuals**

A four-column table with header cells Category, Goal, Success Criteria and Owner(s), all in bold white Calibri on medium blue. Row 1 (light blue fill): Category 'Training'; Goal '% of eligible team members certified to perform check in'; Success Criteria '100%'; Owner(s) blank. Row 2 (pale blue-grey fill): Category blank; Goal '% of eligible team members certified to perform finger sticks'; Success Criteria '90%' followed by a bullet list: 'Must work more than 20 hours per week', 'Must be able to perform service using both hands', 'Religious reasons', 'Medical reasons', 'Position code is:' with two sub-bullets 'Perform: RxM, HG, Rx Tech' and 'Check-In Only: SFL, ASMT, ASM, MGR', then bold 'Action Item:' with bullet 'Need to work with ER to define eligible reasons'; Owner(s) blank. Also a small Walgreens logo at bottom left: a red rounded speech-bubble shape with a white script 'W' and a small red tail at the lower left, 46 by 36 pixels, shown at about 3.6% by 5.0% of the slide.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white slide with a solid medium blue header band (#56A0D3) across the top of the slide. Title in white ArialMT regular sans-serif, sentence-style capitalisation (title case words), about 4.4% of slide height. Table header text is white Calibri Bold about 3.7% of slide height. Table body text is black Calibri regular about 3.0% of slide height, sub-bullets about 2.2%. Table cell fills: header #56A0D3, row 1 #D0DFEE, row 2 #E9EFF7, separated by thin white gridlines. Bullets are black round dots with indent. Footer text is black Arial about 1.5% of slide height. Shapes are square-cornered with no shadows or gradients.

A footer sits at the bottom left: a logo at about 6% from the left and 93% from the top, the copyright line starting at 14.5% and a Bates label centred near 45% at the bottom.

**Visuals**

A table with four columns and six rows including the header. Header row (Category | Goal | Success Criteria | Owner(s)) on #56A0D3 with white bold Calibri text. **Layout**

Full-width blue title band across the top about 16.7% of slide height, with the title left-aligned at about 6% from the left edge and vertically centred in the band. Body bullets are left-aligned in one column starting at about 8.3% from the left, with sub-bullets indented to about 10.1%, running from about 20% to 61% of slide height. Footer strip at bottom: Walgreen logo bottom-left, copyright line starting at about 14.5%, Bates-style line centred near 45%, and page number bottom-right at about 93% x.

**Visuals**

Walgreens logo (red speech-bubble shape with white script 'w' letter) at bottom-left, about 46 by 36 pixels, sitting at x 6.1%, y 93.3%, size 3.6% by 5.0%. No charts, photographs or other illustrations.

**Design**

Background white #FFFFFF. Title band is a solid flat light blue #56A0D3 across the top with no gradient or shadow, edge to edge, height 16.7% of slide. Title text white #FFFFFF in Arial (ArialMT) regular, sans-serif, about 4.4% of slide height, sentence case. Body bullets in Arial regular, sans-serif, about 3.7% of slide height, primary bullets in dark grey #58595B and secondary sub-bullets in lighter slate grey #6A737B at about 3.3% to 3.7% of slide height. Bullets are round dots. Footer copyright text in Arial about 1.5% of slide height, black #000000; page number in grey #898989, about 1.5% of slide height. Line spacing is generous, roughly 1.4 times the font size. No corner radii, shadows or rules.

**Layout**

Full-bleed blue title band across the top with the title left-aligned at about 5% from the left edge and vertically centred in the band. Below it a full-width borderless table with a bold header row, three columns (Timing, Target Date, Training event) starting at about 2.4% from the left edge, alternating teal and white row bands, thin teal rules above the header, below the header and at the table bottom. Footer at the bottom left with the Walgreens logo, copyright line, Trial Exhibit line and page number 35 at about 88% across.

**Visuals**

Table with three columns and seven data rows, plus header row. Header: Timing | Target Date | Training event. Rows: T-10 | 8/6 | Finalize California Wave 1 store selection for 4 stores. T-9 | 8/11-8/22 and 8/11-8/15 | Conduct Field Webinars; Assess store readiness and start construction process. T-8 | 8/11-9/12 and 9/15-10/3 | Hire Bots/HG; Complete Bot/HG Training. T-5 | 9/8-9/26 | Conduct Check-In Training for existing WAG Techs. T-2 | 9/29-10/3 and 9/29-10/10 | Store Manager and Pharmacy Manager expectations/reminder/”coming very soon” Compass communications to stores in the Wave (with copy to DM and RXS); TIFT Checklist for Pre-Launch Activities. T-1 | 10/13-10/17 | Reminder to DMs, RXSs, and CLs in districts/communities with stores going live in the wave: cascade or otherwise communicate to all stores in your area about new capability in additional stores coming online in one week; 5 Minute Meeting scripts to all stores in wave, with instructions on using on or near go-live. T (Go Live) | 10/21 | MVP recognition via email communications about latest wave of stores going live. Recognition of MGRs, RXMs, Pharmacists, Techs, and other field personnel involved in stores launching successfully; Theranos Go-Live in 4 stores. Row bands alternate: teal tint on T-10, T-8, T-2 and T (Go Live) rows, white on T-9, T-5 and T-1 rows. Logo: Walgreens mark, a red speech-bubble shape with a white script 'w' in the centre, bottom left, about 3.6% wide.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title band and table accents are light blue and mint teal. Title text is white Arial regular, about 4.8% of slide height (cap height about 3.4%). Body text is black Arial regular, about 2.2% of slide height; header row and timing labels are Arial bold. Footer text is black Arial regular at about 1.5% of slide height. Header band colour #58A1D3. Row tint #75C7B9 at 20% opacity, which renders as about #DDF3EF on white. Rules are thin teal #75C7B9 lines about 1 px. Logo red #E31837 with white script. No corner radii, shadows or gradients.

### Slide 36

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A Gantt-style timeline lays out parallel workstreams across months with dated milestones, making a complex rollout readable at a glance.
- Review: The survey charts on slide 5 and the timeline on slide 36 are legible and carry their point, but they sit among many slides with weak hierarchy, no takeaway titles and unexplained content such as the Venn on slide 21.
- Worth keeping: Keep the survey bar charts on slide 5 with their direct value labels and average per chart, and the color-coded workstream bars of the timeline on slide 36.

**Layout**

Full-width blue header band across the top holds the left-aligned white title, with a thin grey-to-white body area below. The main content is a Gantt-style timeline: a grey month bar (July to Oct) spans the top, a bold '2014' sits at the left margin, phase labels with pennant markers run down the left column, and coloured bars with dates are placed on a time axis to the right. A red star marks Go Live at the right edge. The footer has the Walgreens logo at lower left, the copyright line beside it, and the grey page number '36' at lower right.

**Visuals**

Gantt-style timeline diagram with no axis chart. Top bar: a rounded grey (#6A737B) band labelled July, Aug, Sept, Oct in white bold Calibri, spanning x 13.4 to 77.0 percent. Left column: six small pennant arrow shapes pointing right, one per phase: Store Selection teal #75C7B9, Construction purple #B890C2, Perform Personnel grey #6A737B, Check-In Training blue #56A0D3, Perform Training green #7AC143, Communications pink #F07285. Bars with text inside: Site Selection (teal #75C7B9, white label, with two white triangle milestones at about 32 and 40.5 percent x, labelled July 28 and Aug 6); Assess Store Readiness (small purple bar, dates Aug 11 and Aug 15 on either side); Design Elements (purple #B890C2 bar, Aug 11 to Oct 3, with a white triangle milestone at its left end labelled Aug 11: Finalize Design Elements on D1); Permits (purple, Aug 18 to Aug 29); Perform Construction (purple, Sept 2 to Sept 30); Hiring WAG Personnel (orange #FC9D3A, Aug 11 to Sept 12); State Regulations (HG & BOP) (grey #6A737B, Aug 11 to Sept 12); WAG Rx Staff, DH, & MGR (blue #56A0D3, Aug 25 to Sept 26); WAG Bot/HG (green #7AC143, Sept 15 to Oct 3); Check-In & Collection Training (green #7AC143, Sept 8 to Sept 26); Communications bar (pink #F07285, spanning most of the width, with white triangle milestones labelled Aug 12 Field Webinar and Sept 23 Launch Readiness). Red star (#FF0000) at right, containing white bold text Go Live Oct 21, overlapping the right ends of the Perform Construction and Hiring areas. Light grey watermark text 'Confidential – Dates / Subject to Change' in the upper right. Walgreens logo: red speech-bubble shape with a white script 'w', at lower left, 46 by 36 pixels.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF body, with a full-width blue #56A0D3 header band across the top 16.7 percent of the slide. Clean corporate slide. Title in white regular ArialMT (sans-serif) on blue #56A0D3 band, roughly 4.8 percent of slide height. Year '2014' and phase labels in bold Calibri, black #000000, about 2.0 to 3.7 percent of height. Date and milestone labels in Calibri, navy #1F497E, about 1.5 percent of height, milestone labels bold. Bar labels in white bold Arial, about 1.5 percent of height. Bars are rounded rectangles with small corner radii, filled solid, no outlines, no shadows, no gradients. Milestone markers are small white upward triangles. Watermark is bold Calibri in pale grey-blue #E2E9EC. Footer copyright in Arial, black, about 1.5 percent; page number in Arial, grey #898989.

### Slide 38

**Review notes**

- Review: Many pages are repeated agenda trackers (slides 15, 16, 19, 24), several are dense tables set in tiny type that cannot be read at presentation size (slides 18, 22, 23), and slides 38 and 39 show clipped or empty-looking layouts.
- Suggested repair: Cut the repeated change-management tracker slides, enlarge or split the stakeholder and matrix tables on slides 17, 18, 22 and 23, and fix the overlapping footers and the clipped table on slide 38.

**Text**

IT Phase Plan / We are here / * Phase 2 had been previously deemed unnecessary / Phase 0 / Phase 1 / Phase 3* / Phase 4 / Software / Theranos App via Internet / Theranos App via Internet / Theranos App via One Patient View search app / Theranos App via Enterprise EHR / Hardware / All Theranos HW / All Theranos HW / Theranos HW with Walgreens peripherals / Walgreens HW and peripherals / Connectivity / Cellular (WIFI) / Walgreens LAN / or other internet option (DSL/Cable) / Walgreens LAN / or other internet option (DSL/Cable) / Walgreens LAN / Functionality / Slow processing speed / Redundant equipment at check-in / Faster processing speed / Reduced dual entry / Easier application access / Search functionality / Quality Reporting / Streamlined, secure data / Eliminate redundant HW / Integration with PARS / Target Date / Current / End of Q3 FY14 / End of Q4 FY14 / TBD / Trial Exh. 1884 Page 0054 / Note: These phases and corresponding scope are subject to change based on open items / 38

**Layout**

A full-width blue header band runs across the top about 16.7% of the slide height, with the white title left-aligned at about 6% from the left edge and vertically centred in the band. Below it, a pasted table image is centred horizontally from about 12.7% to 91.5% of the width, with a 'We are here' marker and a footnote sitting above its top edge. The Walgreens logo sits bottom-left, the source line is centred near the bottom, the bold note is along the bottom edge, and the page number is at bottom-right.

**Visuals**

One table image, not a native table, with a 5-row by 5-column grid (blank header corner, then Phase 0, Phase 1, Phase 3*, Phase 4 across the top; Software, Hardware, Connectivity, Functionality, Target Date down the left). Cell values: Software row: Theranos App via Internet | Theranos App via Internet | Theranos App via One Patient View search app | Theranos App via Enterprise EHR. Hardware row: All Theranos HW | All Theranos HW | Theranos HW with Walgreens peripherals | Walgreens HW and peripherals. Connectivity row: Cellular (WIFI) | Walgreens LAN / or other internet option (DSL/Cable) | Walgreens LAN / or other internet option (DSL/Cable) | Walgreens LAN. Functionality row: Slow processing speed; Redundant equipment at check-in | Faster processing speed | Reduced dual entry; Easier application access; Search functionality; Quality Reporting | Streamlined, secure data; Eliminate redundant HW; Integration with PARS. Target Date row: Current | End of Q3 FY14 | End of Q4 FY14 | TBD. Four green block arrows with dark teal outlines point rightward: one from the Software Phase 1 cell into Phase 3*, one from Software Phase 3* into Phase 4, one from Hardware Phase 1 into Phase 3*, one from Hardware Phase 3* into Phase 4, and one from the Connectivity Phase 0 cell into Phase 1. A yellow five-point star with a dark outline sits on the top edge of the Phase 1 column, next to the 'We are here' label. Grid lines are thin dark grey. The Walgreens logo is a red speech-bubble-style mark with a white stylised 'w' script, in the bottom-left corner.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF, with a medium blue #56A2D3 band across the top 16.7% of the slide. White background. Header band is a flat medium blue, about #56A2D3, with the title in white Arial regular, sentence case, roughly 4.8% of slide height. Table text is black or dark grey (#1A1A1A) Arial, with row and column headings in bold, body text about 2% of slide height. Accents are a yellow star #F5E61E, green arrows #8DC63F with dark teal outline #2E6B6B, and the Walgreens red #E31837. Grid lines are thin grey #555555. Page number is grey #898989 in Arial about 1.5% of height. No shadows, gradients or rounded corners apart from the logo.

### Slide 39

**Review notes**

**Text**

Phase 3 Scope / Capabilities prioritized based on criticality for expansion past 40 stores / Priority 1 and 2 items are: / Financial reconciliation (including migrating from v1 to V2 barcode) / Sharing of Patient data (demographics and insurance) between / Theranos/WAG / Implement single sign on (SSO) capabilities between Walgreens and the / Theranos application / Integrate Theranos work queue with the IC+ Phlomometer / POS integration (allowing Theranos to invoke an API when the copay is $0) / Trial Exh. 1884 Page 0055 / 39

**Layout**

A full-width blue title band occupies the top sixteen point seven percent of the slide, with the title left-aligned at about six percent from the left edge and vertically centred in the band. Below it, a single left-aligned bulleted body column starts at about six percent from the left and runs down the upper-middle of the slide, with the two top-level bullets flush left and the four sub-bullets indented about one and a half percent further; the lower third is empty. A Walgreens logo sits bottom-left at about six percent from the left, a centred small black Bates-style footer label 'Trial Exh. 1884 Page 0055' sits near the bottom centre, and the slide number '39' sits bottom-right at about ninety-three percent from the left.

**Visuals**

A Walgreens logo at bottom left: a red speech-bubble-shaped badge with a white stylised lowercase 'w' script mark, with a small square tail at its lower-left corner. No other images, charts, tables or photographs are present.

**Design**

Background is plain white. The title band is a flat medium sky-blue fill approximately #58A0D3 spanning the full slide width, with no gradient, shadow or border. Title text is white #FFFFFF in a regular-weight Arial-style sans-serif (ArialMT), title size about 4.8 percent of slide height. Body text is Arial regular, about 4.4 percent of slide height, in two greys: top-level bullets #58595B and the sub-bullets #6A737B. Footer label is Arial in black #000000 at about 1.5 percent of slide height. Slide number is Arial in grey #898989 at about 1.5 percent. Body bullets are solid round bullet dots with generous line spacing for wrapped lines; no corner radii, shadows or gradients are used.
